frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:3:/**
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:4: * SolvencyGauge — animated semicircular SVG arc gauge.
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:5: *

frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:11:export default function SolvencyGauge({ score = 0, size = 200, showLabel = true }) {
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:12: const arcRef = useRef(null);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:14: // ── Derived values ───────────────────────────────────────────────
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:15: const clampedScore = Math.min(100, Math.max(0, score));
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:17: // Color based on score band
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:18: const arcColor =
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:19: clampedScore >= 70 ? '#10b981' // green
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:20: : clampedScore >= 40 ? '#f59e0b' // yellow
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:21: : '#ef4444'; // red
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:23: const rating =
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:24: clampedScore >= 70 ? 'Strong'
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:25: : clampedScore >= 40 ? 'Developing'
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:26: : 'At Risk';
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:28: // ── SVG geometry ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:29: const stroke = size * 0.075; // arc thickness scales with size
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:30: const radius = (size - stroke) / 2; // radius of the arc centre-line
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:31: const cx = size / 2;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:32: const cy = size / 2;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:34: // The arc spans 200° (from 190° → 350° going clockwise)
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:35: // = starting from bottom-left, sweeping right across the top, ending bottom-right
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:36: const startAngle = 190; // degrees
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:37: const sweepDeg = 200; // total arc degrees
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:39: // Convert degrees → radians helpers
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:40: const toRad = (deg) => (deg * Math.PI) / 180;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:42: const pointOnCircle = (angleDeg) => ({
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:43: x: cx + radius * Math.cos(toRad(angleDeg)),
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:44: y: cy + radius * Math.sin(toRad(angleDeg)),
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:45: });
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:47: const start = pointOnCircle(startAngle);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:48: const end = pointOnCircle(startAngle + sweepDeg);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:49: const largeArc = sweepDeg > 180 ? 1 : 0;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:51: // Full arc path (background)
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:52: const bgPath = [
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:53: `M ${start.x} ${start.y}`,
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:54: `A ${radius} ${radius} 0 ${largeArc} 1 ${end.x} ${end.y}`,
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:55: ].join(' ');
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:57: // Foreground arc (score portion)
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:58: const scoreSweep = (clampedScore / 100) * sweepDeg;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:59: const scoreEnd = pointOnCircle(startAngle + scoreSweep);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:60: const scoreLarge = scoreSweep > 180 ? 1 : 0;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:62: const fgPath = [
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:63: `M ${start.x} ${start.y}`,
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:64: `A ${radius} ${radius} 0 ${scoreLarge} 1 ${scoreEnd.x} ${scoreEnd.y}`,
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:65: ].join(' ');
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:67: // ── Arc length for animation ──────────────────────────────────────
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:68: // Approx circumference of full 200° arc
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:69: const fullArcLength = (sweepDeg / 360) * (2 * Math.PI * radius);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:70: const scoreArcLength = (clampedScore / 100) * fullArcLength;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:72: useEffect(() => {
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:73: if (!arcRef.current) return;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:74: const el = arcRef.current;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:75: const totalLen = el.getTotalLength?.() ?? fullArcLength;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:77: el.style.strokeDasharray = totalLen;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:78: el.style.strokeDashoffset = totalLen;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:80: // Trigger animation frame after mount
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:81: const raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {

frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:83: el.style.strokeDashoffset = totalLen - scoreArcLength;
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:84: });
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:86: return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:87: }, [clampedScore, fullArcLength, scoreArcLength]);
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:89: // Font sizes scale with gauge size
frontend/src/components/SolvencyGauge.jsx:90: const scoreFontSize = size * 0.22;
